# All closed non-PR desktop issues — complete historical audit

## Scope and evidence

This report covers **all 792 closed, non-pull-request issues** returned for [kiwix/kiwix-desktop](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues?q=is%3Aissue+is%3Aclosed), not the former 15-recent-closed sample. Every issue has a disposition and concrete acceptance below, including dependency, documentation, release, declined, duplicate, stale and external-product items. Closed does **not** mean fixed.

- Retrieval began **2026-10-29T17:07:18.592012+00:00**; final evidence assembly **2026-10-29T17:23:11.657175+00:00**. These are live retrieval timestamps, not an invented midnight historical cutoff.
- REST issues endpoint, `state=closed&sort=created&direction=asc&per_page=100`: **14 nonempty pages, 1,380 returned records**, followed by **page 15 empty**; **792 issues, 588 excluded PR records, 792 unique issue numbers**. Each exact request URL, page timestamp, complete object, numeric/node identity and canonical SHA-256 is retained.
- Independent [issue-only search](https://api.github.com/search/issues?q=repo%3Akiwix%2Fkiwix-desktop+is%3Aissue+is%3Aclosed&per_page=1): **total_count=792, incomplete_results=false**. Search was an independent census check, not the pagination source or a capped sample.
- Complete bodies: **581,501 characters**. **13 issues have no body**; absent/image-only details are not invented.
- **727 complete comment threads, 4,086 comments, 1,372,939 comment-body characters**, with full pagination and original author/URL/timestamp records. Counts match the issue records in this snapshot.
- **792 complete timelines, 14,825 events**, including closure/reopening, referenced commits and source PR metadata. Timeline backlinks are not automatically fixes.
- Full-text body/comment reading used **40 structured model-assisted batches without body/comment truncation**. **339 issues with timeline PR references** underwent **37 further linked-fix reconciliation batches**, reading available full PR bodies, non-translation file patches and discussion comments from the concurrent complete Desktop PR audit; selected inline review threads were also available. Direct manual source/diff review underpins the focused findings below. Initial discussion-only analysis is explicitly superseded by the reconciliation where present.
- The [full PR audit raw evidence](desktop-all-closed-prs.raw.json.xz) has **590 closed PRs**, including ghost-author #28/#1388 omitted from the issues endpoint's 588 PR records. This endpoint discrepancy does not change the independently corroborated **792 genuine issues**. Neither 588 nor 590 is presented as an issue count.
- Earliest returned issue creation: **2016-06-08T13:02:26Z**; latest creation **2026-10-15T07:14:23Z**; latest closure **2026-10-28T18:21:07Z**. Issue numbers are not a contiguous issue-only sequence because PRs share numbering.

**Raw deliverable:** [desktop-all-closed-issues.raw.json.xz](desktop-all-closed-issues.raw.json.xz), SHA-256 **`5aab581af4180bf2a08185d82e328dbfd54de80bb922f23458827c00643d0423`**. Its upstream objects, generated analyses, source observations and evidence limitations are separate fields. Relevant primary PR/file/discussion records are additionally copied into `supplemental.reviewed_pull_requests`; other complete PR evidence stays in the sibling raw file, with its assembly identity recorded. External dependency fixes are not represented as an all-history audit of those other repositories; unavailable external/binary patches remain explicitly unreviewed.

## What current coverage means

**No app build, test suite, linter, formatter, emulator, simulator, browser, OS-permission, transfer or package execution was performed by this audit.** This is evidence acquisition and source/behavior analysis. None of the 792 rows is an instruction to close an issue in this rewrite. Tauri/Rust is not proof of safety, accessibility, media codecs, archive compatibility or packaging.

The existing [ISSUES coverage document](../ISSUES.md) and [initial Desktop report](desktop-issue-audit.md) provide current implementation context and separately recorded older runtime observations; they were **not** used to imply historical census completeness. They intentionally remain unchanged by this task. Main integration may change source concurrently: the findings are pinned observations, not assertions about any later edits.

- **Missing / partial:** concrete source differences G1–G5 below.
- **Source mechanism observed:** narrowly reviewed protection or behavior, with the named residual acceptance still required.
- **Mapped:** existing subsystem reference identifies where a requirement belongs, **not** a source audit proving that every clause is implemented.
- **Runtime-covered:** none newly established here. Previously recorded runtime evidence in [ISSUES](../ISSUES.md#exercised-scenarios-and-boundaries) is limited to its exact scenarios and baseline; it cannot certify historical fixtures or later edits.
- **Nonapplicable / external / declined:** old Qt mechanisms, upstream release housekeeping, producer defects and explicitly rejected enhancements are classified rather than silently discarded. Requested aspirations are not mislabeled shipped features.

## Focused applicable gaps and acceptance

### G1 — Missing marker-based portable mode and automatic portable discovery

Evidence: [#1166](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/1166), [#630](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/630), [#461](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/461); merged [#1179](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1179), [#1208](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1208), [#1209](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1209). Their actual diffs were reviewed, including the later removal of executable-adjacent library discovery outside portable mode.

At the pinned source, [`lib.rs` Options/parse_options/data_dir](../../src-tauri/src/lib.rs) chooses an explicit `--data-dir` or ProjectDirs; no executable-adjacent `.portable` activation/default `data/` discovery exists. Explicit profile selection and manually configured monitoring do not implement the prepared-USB workflow. **Missing feature**, not a claim that ordinary profile storage is broken.

**Observable acceptance:** a marker-bearing portable package uses its relative `data/` for app/library/session/configuration defaults, fixes downloads/monitoring to that directory as the accepted follow-up does, discovers prepared ZIMs without manual XML creation, and remains independent of an installed host profile. Move the USB to another machine and verify links/content and intended persistence. Measure actual framework/cache writes before asserting no-host-write isolation. Do **not** invent a general sandbox for arbitrary user-selected external files: upstream review deferred that stricter policy; read-only mode and portable mode are distinct.

### G2 — Missing app-owned remembered import-picker directory

Evidence: [#976](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/976), merged [#1058](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1058). Final upstream code stores `zim-import-dir` only after a nonempty picker selection, initially falling back to OS Downloads and then current directory; the proposed settings preference was rejected.

Pinned [`api.ts` chooseFiles](../../src/api.ts) calls native `open` without `defaultPath` or saving its selected parent. This is **missing explicit persistence**, not runtime proof that an OS dialog never remembers incidentally.

**Observable acceptance:** select a ZIM from a nondefault directory, restart and invoke Ctrl+O: it begins in that directory on each advertised desktop platform. Cancel does not overwrite the remembered directory. Explicit CLI/file-association opens need not change this picker-specific state. No extra settings UI is required by the accepted upstream behavior.

### G3 — Missing capability-aware online filtering for unreadable SW archives

Evidence: [#780](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/780). Unmerged [#870](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/870) is **not** a delivered fix; merged [#887](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/887) originally applied `notag=_sw:yes`. Later merged [#965](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/965) removed that query application while retaining the unused exclusion variable: this is a **historical upstream regression**, not a claim current Qt Desktop still filters.

Pinned [`server.rs` archive_request](../../src-tauri/src/server.rs) explicitly rejects archive Service Worker scripts. [`Catalog.tsx` catalogURL/filterURL/entries](../../src/components/Catalog.tsx) and [`catalog.rs` parsing](../../src-tauri/src/catalog.rs) neither exclude nor compatibility-gate `_sw:yes` entries. Consequently the source can offer unsupported legacy content as downloadable without the historical compatibility protection.

**Observable acceptance:** the online acquisition path must not misleadingly offer known unsupported `_sw:yes` archives as readable; supported Zimit2 content remains available, and custom feeds/pagination do not bypass the chosen capability policy. Upstream accepted online filtering, **not** forbidding local legacy imports. [#487](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/487) was closed because Zimit2 eliminated its SW dependency, not because legacy SW replay shipped. Legacy replay support is therefore an explicit compatibility choice, not falsely claimed upstream parity.

### G4 — Partial speech-rate parity

Evidence: [#1299](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/1299), merged [#1317](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1317). The accepted implementation includes 0.25–2.00 choices, `<`/`>` controls and per-language saved rates; its exact audible multipliers/pitch quality are not proved by the Qt parameter.

Pinned [`Reader.tsx` speech panel](../../src/components/Reader.tsx) exposes 0.5–2 and stores a single rate/voice through [`store.rs` Settings](../../src-tauri/src/store.rs); [`reader-bridge.js` speak](../../src/reader-bridge.js) applies the supplied rate when speaking. Adjustable TTS is **source-present**, but the accepted range, per-language restoration and speed shortcuts are not equivalent. Backend validation already permits 0.25, which does not make that setting reachable from the observed UI.

**Observable acceptance:** expose the retained slower range and speed controls, persist independent language preferences through switching/restart, and define the behavior of changing speed during speech (upstream restarts the text). Verify actual OS voices/rate and availability on each supported platform; browser API existence is not audible acceptance. A continuous slider or explicit pitch compensation was a proposal, not the exact final upstream contract.

### G5 — Dedicated PDF Save As is not system Print-to-PDF

Evidence: [#77](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/77), merged [#1134](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/1134); old [#228](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/pull/228) was unmerged. The final implementation makes Ctrl+S open a save picker and directly produces PDF for HTML, saves directly displayed non-HTML bytes, and remembers the last save directory.

Observed [`Reader.tsx`](../../src/components/Reader.tsx) separates article export/Ctrl+S from “Print / save as PDF” using the system print dialog; [`exports.rs`](../../src-tauri/src/exports.rs) implements original-resource/MHTML export, not a dedicated PDF writer. Earlier picker source passes suggested filenames, not a remembered save directory. This is **partial capability**, not missing all export or all PDF access. The print alternative accepted for [#749](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/749) must not silently replace the later dedicated #77 behavior in a strict superset claim.

**Observable acceptance:** dedicated Save As goes straight to a file picker, chooses a safe title/type-appropriate name, starts in Documents or the last existing save directory, and produces a readable PDF without requiring system printer selection. Saving a displayed media/file resource preserves its bytes/type, not the entire ZIM or a PDF of the wrong surface. Verify save failure/cancel/read-only behavior without claiming a system dialog supplies an independent exporter.

## Explicit non-gaps and scope boundaries

- [#61](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/61) originally wished for browser HTML bookmarks; merged #1163 delivered **Kiwix XML import/export**. Current migration JSON/XML is not falsely called missing solely for omitting the original HTML wish; exact round-trip/deduplication acceptance remains.
- [#107](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/107) cross-language article equivalence depends on producer mappings and was redirected, not shown implemented. UI translation is not cross-archive Fish→Poisson mapping.
- [#459](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/459) book-like exhaustive linear traversal was declined; arbitrary archive order is not an invented required feature.
- [#508](https://github.com/kiwix/kiwix-desktop/issues/508) incremental archive updates were explicitly unavailable; a full replacement download is not incorrectly labeled incremental.
- Service Workers, background daemon persistence, Hotspot/Imager purchase support, Qt-specific dependency fixes, old upstream release numbers and optional distribution channels are separately classified. A LAN reader server is not a Wi-Fi appliance, a captive portal, or a persistent background service.

Rows use the strongest available reconciled outcome. “Implemented upstream” means a relevant merged diff/reference, **not** runtime-certified in this rewrite. Required acceptance distinguishes accepted behavior from an unshipped aspiration; conditional cases remain conditional. Named comments and PR links are primary evidence, while every complete original thread remains in raw JSON.

Save uses a file picker without automatically launching the file; Open downloads to a unique OS-temporary file retaining its extension, then requests opening through the OS association. Report an opening error when the OS-opening API returns failure. ## Evidence limitations and handoff

The complete issue census, body/comment reads and per-item classification are delivered. This task did not edit app code or shared ISSUES/CHANGELOG. Main integration owns acceptance and any remediation. The focused source gaps were handed off as discovered, with exact source/interface references. G1–G5 are findings against the pinned observations, so later integration should update their status only from its actual changed source/runtime evidence.

External repository bodies/timeline links can establish the existence or claimed purpose of a merged fix without supplying its complete patch; such rows explicitly retain that limitation. Image attachments, unavailable binary patches and unrun platform scenarios are not invented evidence. The audit does not claim all historical defects fixed, every desired enhancement required, every release channel present, or any all-platform runtime certification.
